# Changelog

All notable changes to Sprout will be documented in this file.

## [v0.17.21] - 2026-08-29

- fix(agent): route CLI auto-resume turns through the REPL loop — wakeup poller resume turns ran on a bare goroutine while the REPL idled in ReadLine, so no turn renderer existed and every streamed chunk hit the PrintExternal fallback (newline per chunk + prompt redraw), rendering prose one token per line with no header/steer panel/turn summary. Interactive CLI now registers a wake fn (SetWakeupWakeFn); InputReader gains Wake/ArmWakeup/ErrWakeupPending with a readability-poll gate so a wake can interrupt an idle read, and the REPL runs the resume through full turn machinery with wakeup-budget accounting. WebUI/headless keep the goroutine path. (fcf79916b)

Fixed: config URLs now point to actual HuggingFace files (model_q4.onnx + model_q4.onnx_data), downloader handles companion data files, tokenizer parses both array and string merge formats and object pre_tokenizer patterns, ONNX runtime auto-discovers bundled shared library. Memory retrieval test confirms correct top-1 ranking for all 3 test queries. (e2ec79763)

- Wire ONNX provider activation into agent embedding setup. When provider mode is "auto" or "onnx", the ONNX model is activated in the background after the embedding manager is created. This enables ONNX-based semantic search, conversation turn embeddings, and memory embeddings. (6c7dffc30)
- Wire ONNX provider activation into agent embedding setup. When provider mode is "auto" or "onnx", the ONNX model is activated in the background after the embedding manager is created. This enables ONNX-based semantic search, conversation turn embeddings, and memory embeddings. (1aede829d)
- SP-034: ONNX Embedding Provider with generic runtime architecture. Adds EmbeddingGemma 300m support via ONNX Runtime for semantic search and memory retrieval. Generic ONNXRuntime base is designed for future local LLM providers (Gemma 3 2B). WASM-safe via //go:build !js constraints with stub types. (2846ae177)
- SP-034: ONNX Embedding Provider with generic runtime architecture. Adds EmbeddingGemma 300m support via ONNX Runtime for semantic search and memory retrieval. Generic ONNXRuntime base is designed for future local LLM providers (Gemma 3 2B). WASM-safe via //go:build !js constraints with stub types. (3fe002049)

- The ProgressMonitor was falsely reporting stale output during long LLM extended thinking sessions and synchronous tool execution. The agent process is alive but produces no stdout during these phases (reasoning tokens are suppressed from terminal output by default). The fix checks proc.poll() before declaring stale: if the process is still running, log an informational heartbeat instead of incrementing stale_warnings. (a93881186)
- The ProgressMonitor was falsely reporting stale output during long LLM extended thinking sessions and synchronous tool execution. The agent process is alive but produces no stdout during these phases (reasoning tokens are suppressed from terminal output by default). The fix checks proc.poll() before declaring stale: if the process is still running, log an informational heartbeat instead of incrementing stale_warnings. (5925dbbd7)

- Inline tool call badges in chat messages for WebUI. Tool call pills now render as inline spans instead of block-level divs, with 10px gap between consecutive badges, 11px font size, and human-readable 2-word tool names. Clicking a badge opens the tool execution details in the contextual sidebar. Updated short tool name mapping (e.g. 'todo write' for TodoWrite) with a fallback that converts underscores to spaces and truncates to 2 words. Added the feature spec to roadmap SP-012. (a074fce92)
- Inline tool call badges in chat messages for WebUI. - Fixes a bug where tool events (tool_start, tool_end, stream_chunk, etc.) were silently dropped in the WebUI when running in daemon mode with a workspace different from the daemon location. The root cause was that seed core's conversation loop published events through the raw EventBus without decorating them with client_id/chat_id/user_id metadata. The WebSocket forwarding logic (shouldForwardEventToConnection) requires this metadata to route events to the correct browser tab, and without it, all non-global events were dropped. The fix creates a single richEventPublisher shared between the seed core agent and the tool registry, ensuring ALL events pass through decorateWithMetadata before reaching the EventBus. (cdf41a9fd)
- Fixes a bug where tool events (tool_start, tool_end, stream_chunk, etc.) were silently dropped in the WebUI when running in daemon mode with a workspace different from the daemon location. The root cause was that seed core's conversation loop published events through the raw EventBus without decorating them with client_id/chat_id/user_id metadata. The WebSocket forwarding logic (shouldForwardEventToConnection) requires this metadata to route events to the correct browser tab, and without it, all non-global events were dropped. The fix creates a single richEventPublisher shared between the seed core agent and the tool registry, ensuring ALL events pass through decorateWithMetadata before reaching the EventBus. (d0001e61c)

## [v0.17.20] - 2026-08-29

- feat: preserve thinking across turns — OpenRouter reasoning_details round-trip (capture on Message.Meta, replay verbatim, persist in history), unified reasoning:{effort} control for catalog-supported models replacing the gpt-oss-only gate, Claude disable-path fix; local preserve_thinking via chat_template_kwargs for Qwen3.6+ (loopback-gated, default on), enable_thinking routed correctly for vLLM/llama.cpp, reasoning_content replay enabled for sprout-local/lmstudio (dbab260ed)
- fix(ci): repair failing gates — gofmt/prettier formatting, stale pricing test (now config-derived), WorkspaceGateModal testids, cloudTxn total-cap test 100MiB base64 timeout (caps now injectable), webui MIME test Vite entry-name regex, Termux-only test environment skips (c9cdc94a8)

## [v0.17.19] - 2026-08-24

- Fix flaky log-capture race in pkg/workflow allowed_paths_test: the 4 log-capture tests used defer Unlock() + t.Cleanup(SetOutput restore), so cleanup ran AFTER the lock released, letting a waiting test install its buffer before the first test's cleanup stomped the global writer — causing intermittent 'got logs: ""' failures under -race. Restore writer/flags inside a single defer that unlocks last. Verified: 25x race iterations + full package pass. (025740c3a)
- fix(shell): wakeup deadline is a heads-up, not a give-up; BPM path honors wakeup_timeout (743c6e12f)

- Mark SP-136 P4 complete in TODO.md — all five SP-136 daemon-first architecture items (P0 file-lock index writes, P1 multi-workspace hardening, P2 lazy daemon auto-start, P3 embedding service via socket, P4 full CLI-on-daemon) are now [x]. (d3d864a61)
- SP-136 P4: Full CLI-on-daemon (agent execution). Adds the agent socket protocol and one-shot CLI routing. (1) pkg/daemon/agent_server.go: AgentServer — Unix socket (0600) serving list_sessions / create_session / switch_session / query (one-shot) / stream_query (newline-delimited events) / execute_tool, with an AgentService interface so the daemon owns agent state. (2) pkg/daemon/agent_client.go + socket_conn.go: AgentClient thin client (Query, StreamQuery, session ops, ExecuteTool; re-dial on stale connection; per-op deadline) and the shared socket connection helper. (3) cmd/agent_socket.go: SharedAgentService adapts the daemon's shared agent (Query → ProcessQuery; sessions map to the agent session ID); startDaemonAgentServer runs the socket in daemon mode; tryDaemonOneShot implements `sprout agent "query"` / `--json` one-shot over the daemon with in-process fallback when the socket is unreachable and SPROUT_DAEMON_AGENT=0 escape hatch. (4) Wired into cmd/agent_modes.go: plain one-shot queries route through the daemon when available; daemon mode hosts the agent socket. (5) Tests: TestAgentSocketGate — full session over the socket (create/switch/list sessions, one-shot query, streaming, tool execution, error propagation); cmd-level tryDaemonOneShot tests (routes through daemon, falls back without socket, disabled, empty query); dial-failure test. Also refactored pkg/embedding createProvider to consult the remote provider factory before MLX/ONNX selection. Records P3 checkbox in TODO.md. Gate: TestAgentSocketGate + TestTryDaemonOneShot pass, go test ./pkg/daemon/... ./pkg/embedding/ ./cmd/... pass, make build-all clean, go vet clean. (9569836f6)
- Merge origin/main into main: integrate SP-134 CoreML/MLX embeddings (3721d4116)
- SP-136 P3: Embedding service via daemon socket. Adds a JSON-over-Unix-socket embedding protocol so CLI processes route embedding operations through the daemon (one model load, one index writer, coordinated inference) instead of loading their own 155MB model. (1) pkg/embedding/remote_provider.go: RemoteEmbeddingProvider implementing EmbeddingProvider (Embed, EmbedBatch, prefix variants, meta handshake for name/dimensions/model-hash, per-op deadline, reconnect-on-stale-connection) plus RemoteClient for QuerySimilar/BuildIndex/CheckDuplicates. (2) pkg/daemon/embedding_server.go: EmbeddingServer — Unix socket listener (0600 permissions), JSON request/response loop with ID correlation, dispatch for meta/embed/embed_batch/query/build_index/check_duplicates; EmbeddingManagerService adapts the daemon's shared EmbeddingManager, serializing index writes so concurrent clients never race the single-writer guard. (3) pkg/embedding/embedding_models.go: public Embed/EmbedBatch/Name/Dimensions on EmbeddingManager; SetProviderFactory seam — initLocked consults the process-wide factory first and silently falls back to in-process ONNX when the socket is unavailable (sprout always works). (4) cmd/embedding_socket.go: maybeEnableRemoteEmbedding wires CLI processes to the socket (skipped in daemon mode / SPROUT_DAEMON=1); startDaemonEmbeddingServer runs the socket server in daemon mode, acquiring managers per workspace via embedding.AcquireManager. (5) Wiring in cmd/agent_command.go + cmd/agent_modes.go. (6) Tests: TestEmbeddingSocketGate — 3 concurrent CLI processes build/query/dup-check the same workspace through one socket; all succeed, one server-side manager/provider, fresh store opens clean with exactly 4 records (zero corruption); plus provider-factory (uses remote; falls back on error) and dial-failure tests. Also hardens TestStartDaemon_DetachedFromCtx to poll instead of a fixed sleep. Records P2 checkbox in TODO.md. Gate: TestEmbeddingSocketGate passes, go test ./pkg/embedding/ ./pkg/daemon/... ./cmd/... pass, make build-all clean, go vet clean. (8861b82a4)
- feat: raise subagent timeouts — 30m default, 1h for orchestrator persona (40c78b387)
- feat(local_llm): Go-native LLM inference via MLX — Qwen3-0.6B prototype (442e2f7d6)
- SP-136 P2 chunk B: wire lazy daemon auto-start + idle reaping into the CLI. (1) cmd/daemon_autostart.go: maybeAutoStartDaemon — async best-effort daemon ensure at CLI startup; skipped when SPROUT_DAEMON=0 (escape hatch, prints notice) or =1/daemonMode; on failure warns and continues in-process (fallback preserved); spawned daemons get SPROUT_DAEMON_IDLE_TIMEOUT=60s so they reap themselves. Wired into the agent command RunE. (2) cmd/daemon_idle.go: reapIdleDaemon — daemon-mode goroutine that cancels the root context after the web UI has had no active clients and no active queries for SPROUT_DAEMON_IDLE_TIMEOUT; explicit `sprout agent -d` daemons unaffected unless the operator opts in. (3) pkg/webui/server.go: exports ActiveClientCount (clients with LastSeenAt inside the window) and ActiveQueryCount for idle detection. (4) pkg/daemon: DaemonSpec.Env applied to the spawned daemon (used to pass the idle timeout); test helper supports SPROUT_DAEMON_HELPER_IDLE_MS to simulate daemon self-reaping. (5) Gate test: TestDaemonFullLifecycle — clean hermetic env: EnsureDaemon starts daemon, client connects + queries via /health, disconnects (no traffic), daemon reaps itself after the idle timeout; plus unit tests for reapIdleDaemon (fires after idle; suppressed by active client/query; stops on external cancel) and maybeAutoStartDaemon no-op paths. Gate: TestDaemonFullLifecycle passes, go test ./pkg/daemon/... and ./cmd/... pass, make build-all clean, go vet clean. (dde2a0a9d)
- SP-136 P2 chunk A: daemon auto-start + lifecycle machinery in pkg/daemon. Adds DaemonSpec (URL, socket path, PID file, timeouts, spawn command, defaults), DetectDaemon (GET /health over TCP then Unix-socket fallback), StartDaemon (detached spawn + health poll until StartTimeout), EnsureDaemon (SPROUT_DAEMON=0 escape hatch -> ErrDaemonDisabled; fast path when already healthy; flock-based PID-file election — winner spawns, losers wait for health; stale PID handled by OS flock release), and DaemonLifecycle (connection Add/Remove, idle shutdown timer, teardown cancellation, single-fire StopFunc). Process detachment via Setsid with Setpgid fallback (mirrors agent_tools pattern) + Windows/fallback stubs. Tests cover detection (HTTP, Unix socket, closed port), election (single winner, loser waits, timeout, concurrent race), escape hatch, lifecycle cycles, and helper-process spawn verification. Review fixes applied: (1) removed function-local sync.Once bug in health.go Start; (2) flaky connection-refused test retries on port reuse; (3) helper cleanup now registered via t.Cleanup so failed tests don't orphan helper processes; (4) makeTestSpec now uses an inert DaemonCommand — the default [os.Executable(), "agent", "-d"] spawned the compiled TEST binary as a real daemon, recursively running the test suite and leaking processes; (5) removed t.Parallel from daemon tests and hardened freePort/helper bind retries for constrained hosts (301-port ephemeral range); (6) fixed TestLifecycleMultipleCycles count accumulation; (7) clearer PID-file lock error. Also records P1 checkbox in TODO.md. Gate: go test ./pkg/daemon/... passes repeatedly (incl. -race), go build ./... clean, no orphaned processes. (a621fa392)

- refactor: remove remaining SP-### tracking markers and trim verbose comments in pkg/agent (1b65601d5)
- SP-136 P1: Daemon multi-workspace hardening. (1) Port assignment: documented the single-port invariant — daemon mode always binds DaemonPort (56000) for ALL workspaces, routing per-workspace via clientContext/chat_sessions; FindAvailablePort (5600x scan) is explicitly restricted to non-daemon interactive instances (cmd/agent_modes.go, pkg/webui/server_utils.go, pkg/webui/ssh_types.go comments). (2) Session isolation: documented and verified the isolation invariants in getOrCreateClientContextLocked and getChatAgent (per-client contexts, per-workspace config dirs, embedding.AcquireManager keyed by indexDir+workspaceRoot, CLI-agent seed gated on default client+chat). (3) Health-check-based graceful degradation: new pkg/daemon package with HealthMonitor (polling /health, consecutive-failure threshold, warning + fallback callback, idempotent Start/Stop) and CheckHealth; /health endpoint already existed on the server. (4) Integration gate test: TestMultiWorkspaceConcurrentSessions — 5 concurrent sessions across 3 workspaces on one server; all complete without hang; client contexts isolated to their workspace; embedding managers shared within a workspace but distinct across workspaces; daemon RSS bounded (measured via /proc/self VmRSS on Linux). Also fixes from review: HealthMonitor.Start used a function-local sync.Once so double-starts raced and double-closed stopDone (panic) — moved once to a struct field; flaky TestCheckHealthConnectionRefused retries on port reuse; test compile fix (strings.Join). Also records the P0 checkbox flip in TODO.md. Gate: integration test passes, make build-all clean, go vet clean, webui/agent/search/embedding/daemon/cmd suites pass. (ceb275fe2)
- fix(embedding): address code review findings for MLX provider (97eebbf75)
- SP-136 P0: File-lock index writes. Adds cross-process flock on indexDir/.build.lock via github.com/gofrs/flock. BuildIndex, UpdateFile, and UpdateFromGitDiff now acquire the lock with a 2s poll timeout; if another process holds it, the build is skipped gracefully (no corruption). Read-only queries take no lock. Falls back to no locking when flock is unavailable (WASM/restricted sandbox, empty IndexDir). Re-entrant lockForBuild uses an atomic flag so nested calls (UpdateFromGitDiff -> UpdateFile) don't deadlock on flock(2). Wire IndexDir into IndexOptions from EmbeddingManager.initLocked. Adds TestConcurrentBuilders (two goroutines building the same index dir, verifies no corruption, valid records JSON, queryable index) and TestBuildIndexSkipsWhenLockHeld (lock held -> errBuildLocked, graceful skip, re-acquire after release). Gate: go test ./pkg/embedding/ -run TestConcurrentBuilders -v passes; make build-all clean; go vet clean; embedding/search/agent/webui package tests pass. (b24c44f6a)

## [Unreleased]

- feat(security): SP-127 M1–M3 filesystem gate promotion — path-tier classification unified into Gate 1; `classifyFileAccess` is Gate 1's single source of truth; all 6 file-touching handlers (`write_file`, `edit_file`, `read_file`, `list_directory`, `write_structured_file`, `patch_structured_file`) migrated to `PrecheckFileAccess` pre-check so Deny paths return typed errors and Allow paths bypass the approval dialog; audit trail added for every allow/prompt/deny verdict via JSONL logging; conformance tests and integration tests extended. M4 (remove old machinery) deferred. See `roadmap/SP-127-filesystem-gate-promotion.md`.

## [v0.16.23] - 2026-07-06

- fix(test): correct non-deterministic progress callback assertion\n\nTestProcessOCRImages_ProgressCallback asserted that progress.completed\nvalues were strictly monotonically increasing in callback order. But\nprocessOCRImagesParallel processes images concurrently — the completion\norder is non-deterministic, so callback N can fire with completed=2\nbefore callback N-1 fires with completed=1. Under -race this ordering\ninversion becomes common (race detector slows execution).\n\nReplaced the monotonic-order assertion with a set-membership check:\nthe completed values must be a permutation of {1,2,3} (each image\nincrements the counter exactly once) and the max must be 3 (all\nprocessed). This is the correct invariant for a parallel\nimplementation.\n\ngo test -race -run TestProcessOCRImages_ProgressCallback -count=10: pass (d2bba696)
